   1++6C41              ; ============================================================================
   2++6C41              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6C41              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6C41              ;
   5++6C41              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6C41              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6C41              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6C41              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6C41              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6C41              ; ============================================================================
  11++6C41              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6C41              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6C41              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6C41              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6C41              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6C41              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6C41
  18++6C41              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6C41                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6C41 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6C44 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6C47 B7                   or      a
  23++6C48 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6C4A C2 89 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6C4D
  26++6C4D                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6C4D 21 46 89             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6C50 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6C53 B7                   or      a
  30++6C54 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6C56 DA 89 6C             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6C59 CA 89 6C             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6C5C 22 A3 6C             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6C5F 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6C60 FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6C62 D2 89 6C             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6C65
  38++6C65 21 46 89             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6C68 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6C6B B7                   or      a
  41++6C6C ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6C6E DA 77 6C             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6C71 CA 77 6C             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6C74 C3 89 6C             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6C77              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6C77                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6C77 3A 86 73             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6C7A FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6C7C C2 89 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6C7F 3A 83 73             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6C82 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6C84 C2 89 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6C87 B7                   or      a
  54++6C88 C9                   ret
  55++6C89
  56++6C89              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6C89 37                   scf
  58++6C8A C9                   ret
  59++6C8B
  60++6C8B              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6C8B              p127_layout_report:
  62++6C8B 21 A5 6C             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size
  63++6C8E CD AE 62             call    p127_puts
  64++6C91 3A A4 6C             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size+1)
  65++6C94 CD CA 62             call    p127_put_hex8
  66++6C97 3A A3 6C             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size)
  67++6C9A CD CA 62             call    p127_put_hex8
  68++6C9D 21 C1 6C             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size_tail
  69++6CA0 C3 AE 62             jp      p127_puts
  70++6CA3
  71++6CA3              p127_kernel_size:
  72++6CA3 00 00                dw      0
  73++6CA5              p127_msg_kernel_size:
  74++6CA5 5B 50 31 32          db      "[P127] KERNEL IMAGE SIZE=0x",0
  74++6CA9 37 5D 20 4B

 491+ 6CDD              ; Keep the remap-critical code/data at the start of the Stage2 image so it
 492+ 6CDD              ; remains inside W1/P5. The preflight below verifies the actual linked bounds.
 493+ 6CDD                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++6CDD              ; ============================================================================
   2++6CDD              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6CDD              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6CDD              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6CDD              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6CDD              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6CDD              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6CDD              ; ============================================================================
   9++6CDD              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6CDD              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6CDD              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6CDD
  13++6CDD              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6CDD              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6CDD              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6CDD              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6CDD              p127_resident_start:
  18++6CDD
  19++6CDD              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6CDD              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6CDD              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6CDD              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6CDD              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6CDD              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6CDD
  26++6CDD              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6CDD              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6CDD              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6CDD              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6CDD              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6CDD 32 67 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6CE0 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6CE1 32 68 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6CE4 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6CE5 32 69 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6CE8 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6CE9 32 6A 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6CEC
  39++6CEC 3A 67 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6CEF FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6CF1 C2 1B 6D             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6CF4 3A 68 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6CF7 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6CF9 C2 1B 6D             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6CFC 3A 69 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6CFF FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6D01 C2 1B 6D             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6D04 3A 6A 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6D07 E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6D09 CA 1B 6D             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6D0C                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6D0C 3A 67 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6D0F FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6D11 CA 1B 6D             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6D14 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6D16 CA 1B 6D             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6D19 B7                   or      a
  58++6D1A C9                   ret
  59++6D1B
  60++6D1B              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6D1B 37                   scf
  62++6D1C C9                   ret
  63++6D1D
  64++6D1D              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6D1D              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6D1D              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++6D1D 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++6D1F 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++6D21 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++6D23 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++6D25 CD DD 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++6D28 DA 65 6D             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++6D2B
  74++6D2B 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++6D2D 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++6D2F 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++6D31 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++6D33 CD DD 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++6D36 D2 65 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++6D39
  81++6D39 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++6D3B 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++6D3D 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++6D3F 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++6D41 CD DD 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++6D44 D2 65 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++6D47
  88++6D47 3E 03                ld      a,3
  89++6D49 06 01                ld      b,1
  90++6D4B 0E 02                ld      c,2
  91++6D4D 16 00                ld      d,0
  92++6D4F CD DD 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
  93++6D52 D2 65 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  94++6D55
  95++6D55 3E 03                ld      a,3
  96++6D57 06 00                ld      b,0
  97++6D59 0E 02                ld      c,2
  98++6D5B 16 01                ld      d,1
  99++6D5D CD DD 6C             call    p127_resident_validate
 100++6D60 D2 65 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
 101++6D63 B7                   or      a
 102++6D64 C9                   ret
 103++6D65
 104++6D65              p127_resident_test_fail:
 105++6D65 37                   scf
 106++6D66 C9                   ret
 107++6D67
 108++6D67              p127_resident_arg_target:
 109++6D67 00                   db      0
 110++6D68              p127_resident_arg_code:
 111++6D68 00                   db      0
 112++6D69              p127_resident_arg_stack:

 118++6D6B              ; W3 ownership / UI quiesce state machine. Software only; no page OUT.
 119++6D6B              P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE EQU 1
 120++6D6B              P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED EQU 2
 121++6D6B              P127_W3_OWNER_UI EQU 1
 122++6D6B              P127_W3_OWNER_NONE EQU 0
 123++6D6B              P127_W3_OWNER_HANDOFF EQU 2

 125++6D6B              p127_w3_init:

 127++6D6D 32 08 6F             ld      (p127_w3_state),a
 128++6D70 3E 01                ld      a,P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 129++6D72 32 09 6F             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 130++6D75 AF                   xor     a
 131++6D76 32 0A 6F             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 132++6D79 B7                   or      a
 133++6D7A C9                   ret
 134++6D7B
 135++6D7B              p127_w3_ui_enter:

 147++6D94 B7                   or      a
 148++6D95 C9                   ret

 150++6D96              p127_w3_ui_leave:

 248++6E4F              ; Save/restore the complete software W3 ownership tuple.
 249++6E4F              ; Snapshot and rollback are RAM-only; they never touch page registers.
 250++6E4F              p127_w3_snapshot:
 251++6E4F 3A 08 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 252++6E52 32 0B 6F             ld      (p127_w3_saved_state),a
 253++6E55 3A 09 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 254++6E58 32 0C 6F             ld      (p127_w3_saved_owner),a

 256++6E5E 32 0D 6F             ld      (p127_w3_saved_busy),a
 257++6E61 B7                   or      a
 258++6E62 C9                   ret
 259++6E63
 260++6E63              p127_w3_rollback:
 261++6E63 3A 0B 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_saved_state)
 262++6E66 32 08 6F             ld      (p127_w3_state),a
 263++6E69 3A 0C 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_saved_owner)
 264++6E6C 32 09 6F             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 265++6E6F 3A 0D 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_saved_busy)
 266++6E72 32 0A 6F             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 267++6E75 B7                   or      a
 268++6E76 C9                   ret
 269++6E77
 270++6E77              ; Recovery tests: reject a competing acquire, preserve a busy UI when
 271++6E77              ; quiesce is refused, roll back a partial HANDOFF acquire, reject a
 272++6E77              ; transition from HANDOFF, then resume and prove UI admission works again.
 273++6E77              p127_w3_recovery_selftest:
 274++6E77 CD 6B 6D             call    p127_w3_init
 275++6E7A
 276++6E7A                      ; Acquisition from UI_ACTIVE/UI must fail without mutating state.

 278++6E7D CD BA 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire

 283++6E8B C2 03 6F             jp      nz,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 284++6E8E 3A 09 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)

 286++6E93 C2 03 6F             jp      nz,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 287++6E96
 288++6E96                      ; A busy UI cannot quiesce; rollback must retain the active lease.

 349++6F0E              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++6F0E              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++6F0E              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++6F0E              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++6F0E              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++6F0E              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++6F0E              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.

 368++6F22 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++6F24 D2 5B 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail

 375++6F2E ED 52                sbc     hl,de

 377++6F33

 380++6F39 B7                   or      a

   1++6F5D              ; ============================================================================
   2++6F5D              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++6F5D              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++6F5D              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++6F5D              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++6F5D              ; ============================================================================
   7++6F5D              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++6F5D              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++6F5D              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++6F5D              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++6F5D              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++6F5D              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++6F5D
  14++6F5D              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++6F5D              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++6F5D              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++6F5D              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++6F5D              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++6F5D              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++6F5D              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++6F5D              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++6F5D              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++6F5D              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++6F5D              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++6F5D
  26++6F5D              p127_irq_init:
  27++6F5D F3                   di
  28++6F5E 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++6F60 32 3F 72             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++6F63 AF                   xor     a
  31++6F64 32 40 72             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++6F67 32 41 72             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++6F6A 32 42 72             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++6F6D B7                   or      a
  35++6F6E C9                   ret
  36++6F6F

 301++711B              ; CALL-based synthetic entry test. It saves the caller's complete register
 302++711B              ; set, loads sentinels into primary/alternate banks, invokes the same entry

   1++7243              ; ============================================================================
   2++7243              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++7243              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4++7243              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++7243              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++7243              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++7243              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++7243              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++7243              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++7243              ; ============================================================================
  11++7243              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++7243              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  13++7243              P127_MEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  14++7243              P127_MEM_PAGE_COUNT    EQU 64
  15++7243              P127_MEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  16++7243              P127_MEM_FREE          EQU 0
  17++7243              P127_MEM_RESERVED      EQU 1
  18++7243              P127_MEM_ALLOCATED     EQU 2
  19++7243

  24++724C 36 00                ld      (hl),P127_MEM_FREE

  26++7250 C9                   ret

  30++7251              ; A zero-length reservation is accepted as a no-op.

  69++7286              ; Reserve ATM video RAM used by the complete Project112 mode set.
  70++7286              ; Project112 ATM710: ZX uses page 5 or 7; ATM640/EGA/Text use page

# file opened: p127_range.asm
   1++73C1              ; ============================================================================
   2++73C1              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++73C1              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++73C1              ; ABI:
   5++73C1              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++73C1              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++73C1              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++73C1              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++73C1              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++73C1              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.
  11++73C1              ; ============================================================================
  12++73C1              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  13++73C1              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  14++73C1              P127_RANGE_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  15++73C1              P127_RANGE_LIMIT         EQU 0100000h
  16++73C1
  17++73C1              p127_range_validate:
  18++73C1                      ; Add the unsigned 32-bit length to the unsigned start.
  19++73C1 21 CF 74             ld      hl,p127_range_start
  20++73C4 11 D3 74             ld      de,p127_range_length
  21++73C7 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  22++73C8 47                   ld      b,a
  23++73C9 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  24++73CA 80                   add     a,b
  25++73CB 32 D7 74             ld      (p127_range_end),a
  26++73CE 23                   inc     hl
  27++73CF 13                   inc     de
  28++73D0 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  29++73D1 47                   ld      b,a
  30++73D2 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  31++73D3 88                   adc     a,b
  32++73D4 32 D8 74             ld      (p127_range_end+1),a
  33++73D7 23                   inc     hl
  34++73D8 13                   inc     de
  35++73D9 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  36++73DA 47                   ld      b,a
  37++73DB 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  38++73DC 88                   adc     a,b
  39++73DD 32 D9 74             ld      (p127_range_end+2),a
  40++73E0 23                   inc     hl
  41++73E1 13                   inc     de
  42++73E2 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  43++73E3 47                   ld      b,a
  44++73E4 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  45++73E5 88                   adc     a,b
  46++73E6 32 DA 74             ld      (p127_range_end+3),a
  47++73E9 D8                   ret     c                       ; unsigned 32-bit addition overflow
  48++73EA
  49++73EA                      ; End-exclusive address must be <= 0x00100000.
  50++73EA 3A DA 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+3)
  51++73ED B7                   or      a
  52++73EE C2 0C 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  53++73F1 3A D9 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+2)
  54++73F4 FE 10                cp      010h
  55++73F6 DA 0A 74             jp      c,p127_range_valid
  56++73F9 C2 0C 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  57++73FC 3A D8 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+1)
  58++73FF B7                   or      a
  59++7400 C2 0C 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  60++7403 3A D7 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end)
  61++7406 B7                   or      a
  62++7407 C2 0C 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  63++740A              p127_range_valid:

# file opened: p127_arena.asm
   1++74DB              ; ============================================================================
   2++74DB              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++74DB              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++74DB              ;
   5++74DB              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++74DB              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++74DB              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++74DB              ;
   9++74DB              ; API:
  10++74DB              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++74DB              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++74DB              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++74DB              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++74DB              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++74DB              ;
  16++74DB              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++74DB              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++74DB              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++74DB              ; ============================================================================
  20++74DB              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++74DB              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++74DB              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++74DB              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++74DB              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++74DB
  26++74DB              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++74DB              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++74DB              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++74DB              p127_arena_init:
  30++74DB 21 C0 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  31++74DE 0E 3F                ld      c,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  32++74E0 06 40                ld      b,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  33++74E2              .p127_arena_init_scan:
  34++74E2 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  35++74E3 B7                   or      a
  36++74E4 C2 EE 74             jp      nz,.p127_arena_init_guard_found
  37++74E7 2B                   dec     hl
  38++74E8 0D                   dec     c
  39++74E9 10 F7                djnz    .p127_arena_init_scan
  40++74EB C3 A1 75             jp      p127_arena_reject
  41++74EE              .p127_arena_init_guard_found:
  42++74EE 0C                   inc     c
  43++74EF 79                   ld      a,c
  44++74F0 FE 40                cp      P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  45++74F2 D2 A1 75             jp      nc,p127_arena_reject
  46++74F5 32 32 76             ld      (p127_arena_low_page),a
  47++74F8 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  48++74FA 91                   sub     c
  49++74FB CA A1 75             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  50++74FE 32 33 76             ld      (p127_arena_page_count),a
  51++7501 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  52++7503 32 34 76             ld      (p127_arena_next_page),a
  53++7506 B7                   or      a
  54++7507 C9                   ret
  55++7508
  56++7508              ; IN B=non-zero page count. OUT A=first page of the new block.
  57++7508              ; Validate the complete block before changing any page state.
  58++7508              p127_arena_alloc:
  59++7508 78                   ld      a,b
  60++7509 B7                   or      a
  61++750A CA A1 75             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  62++750D 3A 34 76             ld      a,(p127_arena_next_page)
  63++7510 90                   sub     b
  64++7511 DA A1 75             jp      c,p127_arena_reject
  65++7514 32 35 76             ld      (p127_arena_first_page),a
  66++7517 4F                   ld      c,a
  67++7518 3A 32 76             ld      a,(p127_arena_low_page)
  68++751B 5F                   ld      e,a
  69++751C 79                   ld      a,c
  70++751D 93                   sub     e
  71++751E DA A1 75             jp      c,p127_arena_reject
  72++7521 48                   ld      c,b
  73++7522 3A 35 76             ld      a,(p127_arena_first_page)
  74++7525 5F                   ld      e,a
  75++7526 16 00                ld      d,0
  76++7528 21 81 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
  77++752B 19                   add     hl,de
  78++752C              .p127_arena_alloc_check:

   1++7639              ; ============================================================================
   2++7639              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++7639              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7639              ;
   5++7639              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++7639              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++7639              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++7639              ;
   9++7639              ; API:
  10++7639              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++7639              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++7639              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++7639              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++7639              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++7639              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++7639              ; ============================================================================
  17++7639              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++7639              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++7639              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  20++7639              P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT EQU 256
  21++7639              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++7639              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh
  23++7639
  24++7639              p127_as_init:
  25++7639 21 DA 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  26++763C 36 FF                ld      (hl),P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  27++763E 11 DB 77             ld      de,p127_as_page_table+1
  28++7641 01 FF 00             ld      bc,P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT-1
  29++7644 ED B0                ldir
  30++7646 B7                   or      a
  31++7647 C9                   ret
  32++7648
  33++7648              ; Bind an allocator-owned physical page. Physical aliases are forbidden.
  34++7648              p127_as_bind:
  35++7648 32 D5 77             ld      (p127_as_logical_temp),a
  36++764B 78                   ld      a,b
  37++764C FE 40                cp      P127_MEM_PAGE_COUNT
  38++764E D2 EC 76             jp      nc,p127_as_reject
  39++7651 32 D6 77             ld      (p127_as_physical_temp),a
  40++7654 5F                   ld      e,a
  41++7655 16 00                ld      d,0
  42++7657 21 81 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
  43++765A 19                   add     hl,de
  44++765B 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  45++765C FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
  46++765E C2 EC 76             jp      nz,p127_as_reject
  47++7661
  48++7661 3A D5 77             ld      a,(p127_as_logical_temp)
  49++7664 5F                   ld      e,a
  50++7665 16 00                ld      d,0
  51++7667 21 DA 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  52++766A 19                   add     hl,de
  53++766B 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  54++766C FE FF                cp      P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  55++766E C2 EC 76             jp      nz,p127_as_reject
  56++7671
  57++7671 21 DA 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  58++7674 06 00                ld      b,0
  59++7676              .p127_as_bind_scan:
  60++7676 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  61++7677 FE FF                cp      P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  62++7679 28 08                jr      z,.p127_as_bind_next
  63++767B 4F                   ld      c,a
  64++767C 3A D6 77             ld      a,(p127_as_physical_temp)
  65++767F B9                   cp      c
  66++7680 CA EC 76             jp      z,p127_as_reject
  67++7683              .p127_as_bind_next:

  69++7684 10 F0                djnz    .p127_as_bind_scan
  70++7686
  71++7686 3A D5 77             ld      a,(p127_as_logical_temp)
  72++7689 5F                   ld      e,a
  73++768A 16 00                ld      d,0
  74++768C 21 DA 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  75++768F 19                   add     hl,de
  76++7690 3A D6 77             ld      a,(p127_as_physical_temp)
  77++7693 77                   ld      (hl),a
  78++7694 B7                   or      a
  79++7695 C9                   ret
  80++7696
  81++7696              ; Remove one logical mapping. Does not release the physical page.
  82++7696              p127_as_unbind:

  84++7697 16 00                ld      d,0
  85++7699 21 DA 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table

   1++7ABA              ; ============================================================================
   2++7ABA              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7ABA              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7ABA              ;
   5++7ABA              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7ABA              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7ABA              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7ABA              ;
   9++7ABA              ; API inputs:
  10++7ABA              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7ABA              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7ABA              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7ABA              ; Outputs:
  14++7ABA              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7ABA              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7ABA              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7ABA              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7ABA              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7ABA              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7ABA              ; ============================================================================
  21++7ABA              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7ABA              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7ABA              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  24++7ABA              P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE EQU 64
  25++7ABA              P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE EQU 0C000h
  26++7ABA

 359++7D12              ; Runtime regression: seed two source pages, copy 32 bytes across both source

   1++803C              ; ============================================================================
   2++803C              ; Project127 ATM Core — pure software page-mapper model
   3++803C              ; Module version: 0.3.0
   4++803C              ; No hardware OUT instructions are permitted in this stage.
   5++803C              ; ============================================================================
   6++803C              P127_MAP_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   7++803C              P127_MAP_VERSION_MINOR EQU 3
   8++803C              P127_MAP_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
   9++803C
  10++803C              P127_MAP_WINDOW_COUNT  EQU 4
  11++803C              P127_MAP_ROM            EQU 0FFh
  12++803C
  13++803C              ; Shadow map reflects the verified bootstrap layout:
  14++803C              ; W0=TR-DOS ROM, W1=RAM P5, W2=RAM P2, W3=RAM P0.
  15++803C              ; This table is descriptive state only; it does not program hardware.
  16++803C              p127_map_init:
  17++803C 3E FF                ld      a,P127_MAP_ROM
  18++803E 32 32 82             ld      (p127_map_shadow),a
  19++8041 3E 05                ld      a,5
  20++8043 32 33 82             ld      (p127_map_shadow+1),a
  21++8046 3E 02                ld      a,2
  22++8048 32 34 82             ld      (p127_map_shadow+2),a
  23++804B 3E 00                ld      a,0
  24++804D 32 35 82             ld      (p127_map_shadow+3),a
  25++8050 B7                   or      a
  26++8051 C9                   ret
  27++8052
  28++8052              ; Encode physical RAM page ID as the ATM dispatcher byte.
  29++8052              ; IN: A=physical page 0..63.
  30++8052              ; OUT: A=7Fh-page; carry=1 for invalid page.
  31++8052              p127_map_encode_page:

  33++8054 D2 2E 82             jp      nc,p127_map_invalid

  38++805C C9                   ret

 177++8168              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 178++8168              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++8168              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++8168              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++8168              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++8168              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++8168              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++8168              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).
 185++8168              p127_map_handoff_prepare:
 186++8168                      ; Keep the target in E, invalidate any older staged request, then
 187++8168                      ; validate the page/window and index the four-byte mask with DE=0:E.

 191++816D 78                   ld      a,b

  19++835E              ; IN: none.
  20++835E              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.

  22++835E              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++835E              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++835E              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++835E              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++835E              p127_handoff_stack_probe:

  28++835F F5                   push    af

   2++83F9              ; Project127 TR-DOS directory reader
   3++83F9              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++83F9              ; Reads track 0 sectors 1..8 via the documented TR-DOS ROM entry #3D13.
   5++83F9              ; Directory snapshot: 128 * 16 bytes at #C800 (reserved host/staging page P0).

   1+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A000              ; Project127 ATM-native debug console, ported from Project126
   3+ A000              ; Source: Project126/atm_host/p126_screen_console.asm
   4+ A000              ; Module version: 0.1.4
   5+ A000              ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG6 80x25; upper 12 rows status, lower 13 rows scrolling log.
   6+ A000              ; Every displayed debug character and static UI label is mirrored to #EF.
   7+ A000              ; Resident placement: #A000, in the Project126 host display window.
   8+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   9+ A000              P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25 EQU 6

  11+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS EQU 13
  12+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_TOP EQU 12

  14+ A000              P126_VIDEO_PAGE3 EQU #7C

  16+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE0 EQU #7F

  30+ A008 00           bios_dbg_write_col: db 0

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret

 129+ A081
 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly
 133+ A081              ; as in Project126's atm_host implementation.
 134+ A081              p126_atm_restore_dosen:

 261+ A163              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.

 269+ A16A DA D3 A1             jp      c,.p126_print_at_denied
 270+ A16D                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 271+ A16D                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.
 272+ A16D E1                   pop     hl
 273+ A16E D1                   pop     de
 274+ A16F C1                   pop     bc

 284+ A17F              .p126_print_at_loop:
 285+ A17F 2A 0D A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_text_ptr)
 286+ A182 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 287+ A183 B7                   or      a
 288+ A184 CA C0 A1             jp      z, .p126_print_at_done
 289+ A187 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 290+ A18A 23                   inc     hl
 291+ A18B 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl

 293+ A191 57                   ld      d, a
 294+ A192 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)

 359+ A206              .p126_console_char_ok:

 361+ A209 3A 04 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_row)

 392+ A24D C3 2F A2             jp      .p126_console_sync_cursor

  93  A40C                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_physmem.asm"
# file opened: p127_physmem.asm
   1+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A40C              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A40C              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A40C              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A40C              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A40C              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A40C
  15+ A40C              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A40C              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A40C              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A40C              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A40C              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A40C              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A40C              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:
  22+ A40C 3E 53                ld      a,'S'
  23+ A40E CD BA AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
  24+ A411 AF                   xor     a
  25+ A412 32 7D AB             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
  26+ A415 32 7E AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  27+ A418 32 7F AB             ld      (p127_physmem_quiesced),a
  28+ A41B 32 80 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  29+ A41E 32 81 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a
  30+ A421 32 82 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  31+ A424
  32+ A424                      ; The host/staging page must be the documented W3 page before entry.
  33+ A424 3A 35 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
  34+ A427 B7                   or      a
  35+ A428 C2 BF A6             jp      nz,p127_physmem_fail_map_state

  48+ A448 3E 02                ld      a,2

  50+ A44D

  56+ A458

  63+ A466 DA C9 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_lease
  64+ A469 3E 01                ld      a,1
  65+ A46B 32 80 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  66+ A46E
  67+ A46E                      ; Preserve one byte from P0 for a non-destructive port-open probe.
  68+ A46E 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
  69+ A471 32 86 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_p0),a
  70+ A474 2F                   cpl
  71+ A475 32 87 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_probe),a
  72+ A478 3E 01                ld      a,1
  73+ A47A 32 82 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  74+ A47D
  75+ A47D                      ; Select allocated page A and write one complemented probe byte.
  76+ A47D 3A 83 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
  77+ A480 CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
  78+ A483 DA CE A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
  79+ A486 3E 01                ld      a,1

  81+ A48B 3A 87 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_probe)
  82+ A48E 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
  83+ A491
  84+ A491                      ; Return to P0. Its saved byte must be unchanged if the OUT worked.
  85+ A491 AF                   xor     a

  87+ A495 DA CE A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map

 194+ A5AA DA CE A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map

 196+ A5AF CD 54 AB             call    p127_physmem_fill_pattern
 197+ A5B2 3A 84 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_b)
 198+ A5B5 CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 199+ A5B8 DA CE A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
 200+ A5BB 16 16                ld      d,016h
 201+ A5BD CD 54 AB             call    p127_physmem_fill_pattern
 202+ A5C0 3A 84 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_b)
 203+ A5C3 CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3

 208+ A5D1 3A 83 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
 209+ A5D4 CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 210+ A5D7 DA CE A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
 211+ A5DA 16 83                ld      d,083h
 212+ A5DC CD 66 AB             call    p127_physmem_verify_pattern
 213+ A5DF DA D8 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_data
 214+ A5E2 3A 84 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_b)
 215+ A5E5 CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 216+ A5E8 DA CE A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
 217+ A5EB 16 16                ld      d,016h
 218+ A5ED CD 66 AB             call    p127_physmem_verify_pattern
 219+ A5F0 DA D8 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_data
 220+ A5F3
 221+ A5F3 3E 32                ld      a,'2'
 222+ A5F5 CD BA AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
 223+ A5F8
 224+ A5F8                      ; Stage 2.19: exhaust the allocator into a private page-ID list.
 225+ A5F8                      ; The first two pages are already listed. Each new allocation is
 226+ A5F8                      ; checked against all prior IDs before it is appended.
 227+ A5F8              p127_physmem_alloc_more:
 228+ A5F8 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 229+ A5FB FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES

 235+ A608 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 236+ A60B 47                   ld      b,a
 237+ A60C 21 8B AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 238+ A60F              p127_physmem_duplicate_scan:

 240+ A610 BA                   cp      d
 241+ A611 CA DD A6             jp      z,p127_physmem_fail_duplicate
 242+ A614 23                   inc     hl

 245+ A61A 5F                   ld      e,a

 259+ A630 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)

 263+ A63B DA E7 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_reserve
 264+ A63E
 265+ A63E                      ; First sweep: fill every allocated physical page with a unique
 266+ A63E                      ; address-derived pattern whose seed includes its physical page ID.
 267+ A63E 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 268+ A641 32 8A AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 269+ A644 21 8B AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 270+ A647              p127_physmem_full_fill_loop:
 271+ A647 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 272+ A648 23                   inc     hl
 273+ A649 32 89 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_current_page),a
 274+ A64C 3E 46                ld      a,'F'

 276+ A651 3A 89 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 277+ A654 CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 278+ A657 DA CE A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map

 286+ A668 3D                   dec     a
 287+ A669 32 8A AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 288+ A66C 20 D9                jr      nz,p127_physmem_full_fill_loop
 289+ A66E
 290+ A66E 3E 46                ld      a,'F'
 291+ A670 CD BA AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
 292+ A673
 293+ A673                      ; Reverse sweep verifies every page after all other pages have
 294+ A673                      ; already been written. This catches aliases and lost page contents.
 295+ A673 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 296+ A676 5F                   ld      e,a
 297+ A677 16 00                ld      d,0
 298+ A679 21 8B AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 299+ A67C 19                   add     hl,de
 300+ A67D              p127_physmem_full_verify_loop:

 303+ A67F 32 89 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_current_page),a
 304+ A682 3E 56                ld      a,'V'
 305+ A684 CD C0 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_page

 307+ A68A CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 308+ A68D DA CE A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
 309+ A690 3A 89 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 310+ A693 EE 5A                xor     05Ah
 311+ A695 57                   ld      d,a

 313+ A697 CD 66 AB             call    p127_physmem_verify_pattern
 314+ A69A E1                   pop     hl

 316+ A69E 3A 8A AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 317+ A6A1                      ; Reinitialize the reverse counter on first entry.
 318+ A6A1 B7                   or      a
 319+ A6A2 20 03                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready
 320+ A6A4 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 321+ A6A7              p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready:
 322+ A6A7 3D                   dec     a
 323+ A6A8 32 8A AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a

 326+ A6AD 3E 56                ld      a,'V'
 327+ A6AF CD BA AA             call    p127_physmem_trace

 329+ A6B2                      ; Verify the allocator still marks every listed page ALLOCATED.
 330+ A6B2 CD 01 A9             call    p127_physmem_check_allocated

 354+ A6DD              p127_physmem_fail_duplicate:

 368+ A6F1              p127_physmem_cleanup:

 371+ A6F6                      ; Always return W3 to P0 before releasing UI ownership.
 372+ A6F6 3A 81 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_mapped)

 377+ A700 DA B9 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 378+ A703              p127_physmem_cleanup_saved:

 397+ A727

 402+ A730 5F                   ld      e,a

 419+ A74E 18 E7                jr      p127_physmem_cleanup_free_loop
 420+ A750              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:
 421+ A750                      ; Reallocation must return the same lowest free page and release it.
 422+ A750 3A 7D AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)

 436+ A771 CD 20 A9             call    p127_physmem_check_free_state
 437+ A774 DA B9 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 438+ A777              p127_physmem_cleanup_return:
 439+ A777 3A 7D AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)
 440+ A77A B7                   or      a
 441+ A77B 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error
 442+ A77D                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A77D                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A77D                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.
 445+ A77D CD 84 A7             call    p127_physmem_replay_page_log
 446+ A780 B7                   or      a
 447+ A781 C9                   ret
 448+ A782              p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error:
 449+ A782 37                   scf
 450+ A783 C9                   ret
 451+ A784
 452+ A784              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A784              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A784              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A784                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A784                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A784                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A784                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.
 459+ A784 CD 11 A0             call    p126_atm_video_init

 461+ A787                      ; Fixed-row probe: if this label is visible, the selected alternate
 462+ A787                      ; text bank (P7 characters / P3 attributes) and PrintAt path agree.

 576+ A8C0              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.

 584+ A8C8 FE 06                cp      6
 585+ A8CA 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ A8CC FE 07                cp      7
 587+ A8CE 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ A8D0 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ A8D2 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ A8D4 FE 01                cp      1
 591+ A8D6 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ A8D8 FE 02                cp      2
 593+ A8DA 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ A8DC FE 03                cp      3
 595+ A8DE 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ A8E0 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ A8E2 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ A8E4 FE 05                cp      5
 599+ A8E6 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ A8E8 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ A8EA              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ A8EA C5                   push    bc
 603+ A8EB E5                   push    hl
 604+ A8EC 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ A8ED 16 00                ld      d,0
 606+ A8EF 21 81 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 607+ A8F2 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ A8F3 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 609+ A8F4 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 610+ A8F6 E1                   pop     hl
 611+ A8F7 C1                   pop     bc
 612+ A8F8 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ A8FA              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:
 614+ A8FA 0C                   inc     c
 615+ A8FB 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ A8FD B7                   or      a
 617+ A8FE C9                   ret
 618+ A8FF              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ A8FF 37                   scf
 620+ A900 C9                   ret
 621+ A901
 622+ A901              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ A901              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ A901 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ A904 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ A905 21 8B AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ A908              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:
 628+ A908 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 629+ A909 5F                   ld      e,a
 630+ A90A 16 00                ld      d,0
 631+ A90C C5                   push    bc
 632+ A90D E5                   push    hl
 633+ A90E 21 81 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 634+ A911 19                   add     hl,de
 635+ A912 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 636+ A913 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 637+ A915 E1                   pop     hl
 638+ A916 C1                   pop     bc
 639+ A917 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail
 640+ A919 23                   inc     hl
 641+ A91A 10 EC                djnz    p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop
 642+ A91C B7                   or      a
 643+ A91D C9                   ret
 644+ A91E              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ A91E 37                   scf
 646+ A91F C9                   ret
 647+ A920
 648+ A920              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ A920              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ A920              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ A920 21 81 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ A923 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ A925 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ A927              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ A927 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ A928 FE 00                cp      0
 657+ A92A 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ A92C FE 01                cp      1
 659+ A92E 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ A930 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ A932 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ A934 FE 03                cp      3
 663+ A936 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ A938 FE 04                cp      4
 665+ A93A 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ A93C FE 05                cp      5
 667+ A93E 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ A940 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ A942 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ A944 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ A945 B7                   or      a
 672+ A946 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ A948 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ A94A              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ A94A 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ A94B FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ A94D 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ A94F              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ A94F 23                   inc     hl
 680+ A950 0C                   inc     c
 681+ A951 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ A953 B7                   or      a
 683+ A954 C9                   ret
 684+ A955              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ A955 37                   scf
 686+ A956 C9                   ret
 687+ A957
 688+ A957              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ A957              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ A957
 691+ A957              ; ============================================================================
 692+ A957              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ A957              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ A957              ; Public ABI:
 695+ A957              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ A957              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ A957              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ A957              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ A957              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ A957              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ A957              ; ============================================================================
 702+ A957              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ A957 C3 C0 72             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ A95A
 705+ A95A              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ A95A 32 B7 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ A95D 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ A960 B7                   or      a
 709+ A961 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ A964 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ A967 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ A968 3A 35 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ A96B B8                   cp      b
 714+ A96C CA 14 AA             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ A96F 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ A972 C3 D6 72             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ A975
 718+ A975              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ A975              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ A975 F3                   di
 721+ A976 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ A979 B7                   or      a
 723+ A97A C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ A97D 3A 35 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ A980 B7                   or      a
 726+ A981 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ A984 CD 9B 6D             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ A987 D8                   ret     c
 729+ A988 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ A98A 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ A98D CD BA 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ A990 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ A992 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ A994 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ A997 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ A99A 32 B6 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ A99D 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ A99F 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ A9A2 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ A9A5 B7                   or      a
 741+ A9A6 C9                   ret
 742+ A9A7              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ A9A7 CD E2 6D             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ A9AA AF                   xor     a
 745+ A9AB 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ A9AE 37                   scf
 747+ A9AF C9                   ret
 748+ A9B0
 749+ A9B0              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ A9B0              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ A9B0 32 B7 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ A9B3 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ A9B6 FE 01                cp      1
 754+ A9B8 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ A9BB 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ A9BE FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ A9C0 D2 14 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ A9C3 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ A9C4 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ A9C6 21 81 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ A9C9 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ A9CA 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ A9CB FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ A9CD C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ A9D0 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ A9D3 C3 40 AB             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ A9D6
 768+ A9D6              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ A9D6              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ A9D6              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ A9D6 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ A9D9 FE 01                cp      1
 773+ A9DB C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ A9DE AF                   xor     a
 775+ A9DF CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ A9E2 D8                   ret     c
 777+ A9E3 3A B5 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ A9E6 B7                   or      a
 779+ A9E7 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ A9E9 3A B6 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ A9EC 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ A9EF              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ A9EF 3A B4 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ A9F2 B7                   or      a
 785+ A9F3 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ A9F5 CD CE 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ A9F8 D8                   ret     c
 788+ A9F9 AF                   xor     a
 789+ A9FA 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ A9FD              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ A9FD 3A B3 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ AA00 B7                   or      a
 793+ AA01 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ AA03 CD E2 6D             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ AA06 D8                   ret     c
 796+ AA07 AF                   xor     a
 797+ AA08 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ AA0B              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ AA0B AF                   xor     a
 800+ AA0C 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ AA0F 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ AA12 B7                   or      a
 803+ AA13 C9                   ret
 804+ AA14
 805+ AA14              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ AA14 37                   scf
 807+ AA15 C9                   ret
 808+ AA16
 809+ AA16              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ AA16              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ AA16              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ AA16 AF                   xor     a
 813+ AA17 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ AA1A 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ AA1D 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ AA20 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ AA23 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ AA25 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ AA28 D2 B0 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ AA2B CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AA2E DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AA31 32 B8 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AA34 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AA37 32 B9 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AA3A CD 75 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AA3D DA AA AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AA40                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AA40 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AA42 CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AA45 D2 A7 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AA48 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AA4B CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AA4E DA A7 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AA51                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AA51 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AA54 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AA57 D2 A7 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AA5A 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AA5C 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AA5F CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AA62 DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AA65 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AA68 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AA69 3A B9 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AA6C B8                   cp      b
 846+ AA6D C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AA70 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AA73 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AA76 DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AA79                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AA79 CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AA7C DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AA7F 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AA80 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AA83 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AA84 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AA87 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AA88 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AA8B DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AA8E 3A 08 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AA91 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AA93 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AA96 3A 09 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AA99 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AA9B C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AA9E 3A 35 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AAA1 B7                   or      a
 868+ AAA2 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AAA5 B7                   or      a
 870+ AAA6 C9                   ret
 871+ AAA7
 872+ AAA7              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AAA7 CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AAAA              p127_physmem_api_test_free:
 875+ AAAA 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 876+ AAAD CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 877+ AAB0              p127_physmem_api_test_fail:
 878+ AAB0 37                   scf
 879+ AAB1 C9                   ret

 890+ AABA              p127_physmem_trace:

 892+ AABD ED 79                out     (c),a

 894+ AAC0
 895+ AAC0              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AAC0              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AAC0              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AAC0              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AAC0 F5                   push    af
 900+ AAC1 C5                   push    bc
 901+ AAC2 D5                   push    de
 902+ AAC3 E5                   push    hl
 903+ AAC4 FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ AAC6 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify
 905+ AAC8 21 12 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_fill_text
 906+ AACB 18 03                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 907+ AACD              p127_physmem_trace_page_verify:
 908+ AACD 21 20 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_verify_text
 909+ AAD0              p127_physmem_trace_page_text:
 910+ AAD0 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 911+ AAD1 B7                   or      a
 912+ AAD2 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_trace_page_value
 913+ AAD4 23                   inc     hl

 916+ AAD9 E1                   pop     hl
 917+ AADA 18 F4                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 918+ AADC              p127_physmem_trace_page_value:
 919+ AADC 3A 89 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 920+ AADF CD F1 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_hex8
 921+ AAE2 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 922+ AAE4 CD 0C AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 923+ AAE7 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 924+ AAE9 CD 0C AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 925+ AAEC E1                   pop     hl
 926+ AAED D1                   pop     de
 927+ AAEE C1                   pop     bc
 928+ AAEF F1                   pop     af
 929+ AAF0 C9                   ret
 930+ AAF1
 931+ AAF1              p127_physmem_trace_hex8:
 932+ AAF1 F5                   push    af
 933+ AAF2 E6 F0                and     0F0h
 934+ AAF4 0F                   rrca
 935+ AAF5 0F                   rrca
 936+ AAF6 0F                   rrca
 937+ AAF7 0F                   rrca
 938+ AAF8 CD 01 AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 939+ AAFB F1                   pop     af
 940+ AAFC E6 0F                and     00Fh
 941+ AAFE C3 01 AB             jp      p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 942+ AB01

 944+ AB01 5F                   ld      e,a
 945+ AB02 16 00                ld      d,0

 950+ AB0C

 953+ AB0F ED 79                out     (c),a
 954+ AB11 C9                   ret
 955+ AB12
 956+ AB12              p127_physmem_trace_fill_text:
 957+ AB12 46 49 4C 4C          db      "FILL PAGE ID=",0
 957+ AB16 20 50 41 47
 957+ AB1A 45 20 49 44
 957+ AB1E 3D 00
 958+ AB20              p127_physmem_trace_verify_text:
 959+ AB20 56 45 52 49          db      "VERIFY PAGE ID=",0
 959+ AB24 46 59 20 50
 959+ AB28 41 47 45 20
 959+ AB2C 49 44 3D 00
 960+ AB30              p127_physmem_trace_hex_table:
 961+ AB30 30 31 32 33          db      "0123456789ABCDEF"
 961+ AB34 34 35 36 37
 961+ AB38 38 39 41 42
 961+ AB3C 43 44 45 46
 962+ AB40
 963+ AB40              ; IN A=physical RAM page ID. Program only W3 (#FFF7), never W0/W1/W2.
 964+ AB40              ; D6=1 selects RAM, D7=0 selects the inverse D0-D5 page ID.
 965+ AB40              p127_physmem_set_w3:
 966+ AB40 32 85 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_arg),a
 967+ AB43 CD 52 80             call    p127_map_encode_page
 968+ AB46 D8                   ret     c
 969+ AB47 01 F7 FF             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT
 970+ AB4A ED 79                out     (c),a
 971+ AB4C 3A 85 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_arg)
 972+ AB4F 32 35 82             ld      (p127_map_shadow+3),a
 973+ AB52 B7                   or      a
 974+ AB53 C9                   ret
 975+ AB54
 976+ AB54              ; IN D=pattern seed. Byte(address)=ROL(address_low) XOR address_high XOR D.
 977+ AB54              ; The same address produces a different expected value at each seed.
 978+ AB54              p127_physmem_fill_pattern:
 979+ AB54 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 980+ AB57 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 981+ AB5A              .p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop:
 982+ AB5A 7D                   ld      a,l
 983+ AB5B 07                   rlca
